# MS.990 Parallel Edition: Canvases 335–364

## Page 364 — IIIF Canvas #335

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0335.JP2` (5858 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0335.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0335.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,650 / 3,400

Recto folio (numbered 364 in top-right box, pencil foliation 165 at bottom-left) containing Early New High German alchemical and iatrochemical recipes in two columns, including instructions on purifying and mortifying mercury, preparing a magistery of marcasite for skin blemishes, and making Vitriol of Mars (iron sulfate).

### Left Column

| Line | German Diplomatic (Original) | English Translation & Editorial Notes |
| :---: | :--- | :--- |
| 1 | den leib purgiert. darfon gibt man 5 gran od[er] | purges the body. Of this one gives 5 grains or |
| 2 | 6 mit roß zucker vermischt. vn[d] trinckt darauf | 6 mixed with rose sugar, and drinks thereafter |
| 3 | 4 leich löffel voll wein, wan man will zu | 4 small spoonfuls of wine, when one wants to go to |
| 4 | bätt gehn. zu morgens schwizt er darauf. | bed. In the morning he sweats upon it. |
| 5 | 2 stundt, etliche braucht man 4 od[er] 5 tag nach | 2 hours; some use it 4 or 5 days one |
| 6 | ein ander, nach ansehen deß kranckhen. es treibt | after another, according to the condition of the patient. It drives |
| 7 | so völlig alle böse fäulle verderbte gifftige | out so completely all evil putrefaction, corrupted poisonous |
| 8 | humores auß dem ib[er] ist in der Chur der solie | humors from the [body]; it is in the cure of the ... |
| 9 | alian ein köstlich exquisit mittel. vn[d] | ... a precious exquisite remedy. And |
| 10 | kan gebraucht werd[en]. in den süch[en] nicht. leme | can be used. In [such] illnesses not; lameness |
| 11 | fieber halb sucht. thut in der gleich 3. 4. 5. | fever, paralysis. Do in the same way 3, 4, 5 |

## Page 366 — IIIF Canvas #336

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0336.JP2` (5988 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0336.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0336.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,650 / 2,850

Page 366 (verso) of MS.990 presents Early New High German alchemical and iatrochemical recipes arranged in two columns, including a salt of gold preparation for liver, spleen, and uterine complaints, an oil of tin (cinnabar/tin) remedy for the womb, a recipe for sugar of lead (Bley Zügflen), and a salt of copper (Sal Veneris) purgative.

### Left Column

| Line | German Diplomatic (Original) | English Translation & Editorial Notes |
| :---: | :--- | :--- |

| 4 | auffstigung der Mutter. mit solcher erzeigung, das ihr | rising of the womb, with such effect that to her |

## Page 367 — IIIF Canvas #337

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0337.JP2` (5856 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0337.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0337.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 2,450 / 3,100

Introduces medicinal and astrological properties of woods and roots, beginning with mistletoe wood, wintergreen, alder wood, oak mistletoe, ash wood (wound-wood), and peony root, detailing harvesting times by solar/lunar signs and therapeutic applications.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Illustration of Mistletoe Tree** (`#xywh=400,500,4556,1400`): Hand-colored ink and watercolor drawing of a tree with a brown trunk and green-tinted foliage standing on a grassy mound, illustrating the section on mistletoe wood.

## Page 368 — IIIF Canvas #338

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0338.JP2` (5939 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0338.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0338.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 2,450 / 3,150

Continues the herbal and wood remedies with tamarisk, elder growing on willow (for epilepsy and falling sickness), elder blossom and bark preparations, gravel wood (lignum nephriticum), sassafras, and mastic wood (lignum lentisci) from Chios.

## Page 374 — IIIF Canvas #339

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0339.JP2` (5891 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0339.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0339.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 2,450 / 3,100

Concludes the treatise on natural and artificial mineral baths, detailing pre-bath purging and dietary regimens, gradual daily bathing schedules, post-bath sweating, and the stillicidium (head-dripping) therapy illustrated by a bather in a wooden tub.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Illustration of Stillicidium (Head-Dripping Bath Therapy)** (`#xywh=400,500,4591,1400`): Hand-colored ink and watercolor illustration showing a person wearing a yellow headband/wreath sitting up to the shoulders in a round, hooped wooden bath tub filled with water, while a red wooden bucket suspended overhead drips water droplets from a brass-colored spout onto the bather's head.

## Page 377 — IIIF Canvas #342

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0342.JP2` (5943 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0342.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0342.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 310

Full-page colored illustration of a 17th-century apothecary and alchemical laboratory, showing an assistant grinding with a mortar and pestle amid shelves of albarelli and glass flasks, flanked by distillation furnaces.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Apothecary and Alchemical Workshop** (`#xywh=900,500,4643,1400`): Full-page colored ink and watercolor illustration depicting the interior of a 17th-century apothecary and alchemical laboratory. Shelves lined with red cylindrical albarelli jars and blue-tinted glass vessels wrap around the corner walls beneath a small paned window and yellow ceiling beam. At center, a young apothecary or assistant with long dark hair, wearing a red-and-gold doublet and yellow breeches, sits on a three-legged stool grinding substances with a pestle in a large mortar resting on a wooden block before a worktable holding jars and flasks. In the left foreground, a retort and receiver sit on a brick furnace; in the right foreground, a smaller alembic distillation apparatus sits over a portable stove and tripod on a checkered stone floor, while a figure peeks through a slightly ajar wooden door on the left.

### Full Page

| Line | German Diplomatic (Original) | English Translation & Editorial Notes |
| :---: | :--- | :--- |
| 1 | 377 | 377 *— Note: Boxed page number in top-left corner; full-page illustration with no body text.* |

## Page 378 — IIIF Canvas #343

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0343.JP2` (5852 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0343.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0343.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 2,400 / 2,100

A rubricated prefatory title introduces a treatise on distilling and preparing medicinal remedies, followed by instructions on digestion and circulation in flasks, cutting glass vessels, per descensum distillation, and distilling human blood. An ink-and-wash illustration at the bottom left (numbered 169) depicts three distillation vessels and a cubic furnace.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**169** (`#xywh=400,500,4552,1400`): Ink and watercolor illustration of three alchemical distillation apparatuses: at left, a round-bottomed retort/flask with a side spout and stopper; in the center, a tall pear-shaped cucurbit with a bulbous cap; at right, a cubic furnace or housing with a small spherical vessel protruding from the top.

## Page 379 — IIIF Canvas #344

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0344.JP2` (5895 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0344.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0344.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 2,400 / 2,300

Continues with chemical and medicinal distillation recipes, including making rectified spirits/acids, distilling honey, rectifying water without effort, distilling human blood, and preparing refreshing lemon, citron, bitter orange, and jasmine waters.

## Page 380 — IIIF Canvas #345

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0345.JP2` (5832 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0345.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0345.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 2,400 / 2,500

Continues the herbal water recipes, then presents several purgative and laxative waters (including the Queen's Water), two wondrous waters of great virtue with an extensive list of therapeutic uses, and two recipes for apoplexy water (Schlagwasser) using lily of the valley.

## Page 381 — IIIF Canvas #346

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0346.JP2` (5920 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0346.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0346.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,400

Page 381 (verso) contains Early New High German medical and alchemical recipes for distilled waters (Wasser, denoted by ▽) treating epilepsy (Fraiß), stroke (Schlag), headache, vertigo, stomach ailments, constipation, colic, and shortness of breath, followed by anise, cinnamon, and stroke waters.

## Page 382 — IIIF Canvas #347

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0347.JP2` (5839 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0347.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0347.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,300

Page 382 (recto) continues the recipe for stroke water, adds an epilepsy water prepared during the waning moon, and then presents a series of compound medical balsams (Schlag Balsam, Kaiser Leopolds Schlag Balsam, Frauen Schlag Balsam, Herzklopfen Balsam, Fluss Balsam, and Mutter Balsam) along with instructions on coloring balsams.

### Left Column

| Line | German Diplomatic (Original) | English Translation & Editorial Notes |
| :---: | :--- | :--- |
| 1 | ℞ aristalagia ʒiβ. diptamo alb. ʒj. g. peonia | Take birthwort 1.5 drams, white dittany 1 dram, peony seeds *— Note: Contains apothecary symbols ℞ and ʒ* |
| 2 | ʒiβ. Cubeb. ʒj. piper. nigr. ʒβ. Visci quorim. | 1.5 drams, cubebs 1 dram, black pepper half a dram, oak mistletoe *— Note: Contains apothecary symbol ʒ* |
| 3 | ʒv. ▽ meluches ℔ij. g. infus. 3 wohn hernach dz: | 5 drams, ... water 2 pounds; infuse 3 weeks, afterwards distill *— Note: Contains apothecary/alchemical symbols ʒ, ▽, ℔* |

## Page 383 — IIIF Canvas #348

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0348.JP2` (5937 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0348.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0348.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,350

Page 383 (verso) concludes the instructions for coloring compound balsams and then presents several sulfur balsam preparations (Balsam Sulphuris, denoted by 🜍), including an asthma balsam, a potent sulfur balsam, a balsam for gangrene/necrosis, Martin Ruland's Balsam Sulphuris for surgical wounds and hemorrhoids, and a Balsam Vitae.

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0349.JP2` (5790 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0349.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0349.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,350

Page 384 (recto) opens with a recipe for a wound basilicon balm for internal and external conditions, followed by a color illustration of a clyster syringe and a new rubricated section on various clysters. The right column contains clyster prescriptions for apoplexy, lethargy, vertigo, head illnesses, and paralysis.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Clyster syringe** (`#xywh=400,500,4490,1400`): Pen-and-ink and watercolor illustration of a yellow-brown cylindrical clyster syringe with a detached pointed nozzle on the left and a plunger handle on the right, resting above a narrow strip of green grass.

## Page 386 — IIIF Canvas #351

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0351.JP2` (5825 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0351.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0351.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,400

Page 386 (recto) concludes the clyster balm recipe, followed by a large watercolor illustration of two pots boiling on a hearth fire with an attendant carrying a jug. Below a red-and-cream decorative divider, a new rubricated section on decoctions begins, including rooster decoctions for belly pain and liver/spleen obstructions, and decoctions for wind, bile, and fumitory.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Two pots boiling over a hearth fire with an attendant carrying a jug** (`#xywh=400,500,4525,1400`): Pen-and-ink and watercolor illustration of two brown earthen pots boiling over a wood fire with red flames and rising smoke on a rectangular masonry hearth; to the right, a standing figure in a grey coat, yellow tunic, and red breeches holds a red pitcher in a doorway. A decorative red-and-cream striped horizontal bar separates the illustration from the text below.

## Page 387 — IIIF Canvas #352

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0352.JP2` (5979 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0352.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0352.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,450

This recto page (numbered 388) features two colored pen-and-ink illustrations of apothecary vessels and wine goblets, alongside recipes for cooling and laxative emulsions ('Emulsian oder Melaun Milch') and medicinal wines ('Tamarisken Wein', 'Laxier Wein').

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Three apothecary vessels on a wooden shelf (Left Column)** (`#xywh=400,500,4525,1400`): Colored pen-and-ink drawing depicting two cylindrical shaded beakers/tumblers and one handled pitcher/jug resting on a tan wooden ledge above the heading 'Emulsian oder Melaun Milch'.

**Goblet with red wine and cylindrical jar on a shelf (Right Column)** (`#xywh=400,500,4525,1400`): Colored pen-and-ink drawing depicting a stemmed glass goblet filled with dark red liquid beside a cylindrical grey jar/box on a tan and reddish-brown shelf above the heading 'Von Ender hierlichen Wein'.

## Page 390 — IIIF Canvas #355

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0355.JP2` (5807 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0355.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0355.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,350

Page 390 opens with a recipe for a laxative wine, followed by a rubricated treatise on the art of targeted purging—explaining how purgatives like agaric act on specific organs (head, liver, spleen, stomach, chest) and how corrective herbs must be added to protect healthy organs. The upper right column features a colored illustration of an apothecary shop, beneath which the text lists simples that draw to and strengthen the head.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Apothecary Shop Scene** (`#xywh=400,500,4507,1400`): Colored pen-and-ink and watercolor illustration occupying the upper half of the right column. Two upper shelves hold rows of red and yellow-and-blue apothecary jars (albarelli); two lower shelves hold red and green jars. Behind a wooden counter draped with a green-and-yellow cloth stands a woman in a pink bodice, white kerchief, and yellow skirt, gesturing toward a bearded man in a brown robe with bare feet who holds a small dark cup. Four red cylindrical jars, a dark square box, and a dark jug rest on the counter.

Page 392 classifies moderate and strong purgatives as well as mild and strong emetics, followed by instructions on recognizing good scammony and its six harmful effects, and the medicinal virtues of larch agaric. The right column describes the preparation of larch agaric troches and summarizes the properties and humoral degrees of turbith, rhubarb, rhapontica, mechoacanna, and hermodactyl.

## Page 393 — IIIF Canvas #358

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0358.JP2` (5967 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0358.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0358.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,100

Page 393 presents an illustrated materia medica entry on five varieties of myrobalans (Belleric, Emblic, Chebulic, Indian, and Citrine) with their humoral properties and dosages, followed by a rubricated section on preparatory syrups for purging bile, phlegm, and melancholic humours.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**1. Myrobalanj Bellerica.** (`#xywh=900,500,4667,1400`): Wash-shaded ink drawing of an oval Belleric myrobalan fruit with a short stem at the top.

**2. Myrobalanj Emplicj.** (`#xywh=900,500,4667,1400`): Wash-shaded ink drawing of a ribbed, segmented Emblic myrobalan fruit.

**3. Myrobalanj Chebula.** (`#xywh=900,500,4667,1400`): Wash-shaded ink drawing of an elongated, pear-shaped Chebulic myrobalan fruit with a stem at the top.

**4. Myrobalanj Indica.** (`#xywh=900,500,4667,1400`): Wash-shaded ink drawing of a smaller, rounded-teardrop Indian myrobalan fruit with a stem at the top.

**5. Myrobalanj Citrinj.** (`#xywh=900,500,4667,1400`): Wash-shaded ink drawing of a large, oval-pyriform Citrine myrobalan fruit with a short neck at the top.

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0360.JP2` (5963 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0360.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0360.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,250

Page 395 contains ten recipes for purging potions and laxative waters—including a precious laxative water digested in a water bath (NB) and solar heat, a tamarind laxative water, and a Viennese laxative water—each introduced with rubricated red-ink headings.

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0361.JP2` (5857 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0361.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0361.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,450

Early New High German medical-pharmaceutical recipes for laxative waters, a bile potion, laxative milk, quince juice, a laxative potion, and several laxative electuaries using senna, rhubarb, jalap, scammony, and manna.

## Page 397 — IIIF Canvas #362

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0362.JP2` (5965 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0362.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0362.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,380

Early New High German medical recipes for laxative lozenges (morselli) and tablets (Zeltel), followed by topical laxative plasters and ointments applied to the belly or navel, including remedies for children.

## Page 398 — IIIF Canvas #363

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0363.JP2` (5843 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0363.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0363.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,350

Continuation of topical laxative ointments followed by numerous recipes for laxative powders (Laxier Pulver) using jalap resin, scammony, cream of tartar, hermodactyl, and turbith, including a child and bile powder attributed to the apothecary of Leoben.

## Page 399 — IIIF Canvas #364

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0364.JP2` (5955 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0364.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0364.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,100

Introduces a secret collection of noble pill recipes with an explanatory discourse on purging the three bodily humors (phlegm, choler, melancholia), followed by an illustration of a pillbox and two pill recipes for strengthening the senses and preserving the body from putrefaction.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Illustration on canvas 364** (`#xywh=900,500,4655,1400`): Watercolor and ink drawing of a round, flat cylindrical yellow-ochre pillbox or apothecary container with a dark lid rim resting on a brown shelf or table surface.
